# ASL Unit Backlog Pass 32 Design: Appendix, the members that hold rule logic

**Status:** Generated 2026-10-05 by script (`appendix.py` of the session) from the rows of 14 read-only readings of `main` at aba0210. It belongs to the [pass 32 design](<ASL Unit Backlog Pass 32 Design.md>), whose section 4 says how the rows were made and which of them I checked myself.

**How to read it.** One row for each member, or each block of a long member (`#1`, `#2`), that holds rule logic outside the Rules project. **Class:** RULE (all of it is rule logic), MIXED (it reads facts and decides a rule in one body), TABLE (rule values). **Moves as:** WHOLE (a pure function, moved as it is), SPLIT (the facts are gathered first and handed to a calculator), SEARCH (the decision is made inside a loop of reads, so the calculator takes a fact reader), STAYS (in Maps, by the design's decisions D8 and D9). **Rules** are the numbers the code itself cites; `uncited` means the code cites none. The facts and the rule numbers are the readers' words, not checked against the rulebook. Members that only read facts or are plumbing (891 rows) are left out.

## The inventory's 222 split rows: what sits outside Rules

Each row of the [rule inventory](<../ASL Rule Inventory A to E.md>) whose Where column names a Rules file and a file of another project. **Outside, by the inventory** is what the Where column names outside Rules. **Outside, by the code** is every member above that cites the rule's number; empty where the code outside Rules cites the number nowhere (the audit named the file by reading, or the member cites a parent rule).
